# Agents · A · part 32

911 entries, Assured Arc Documentation to audio-architect. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **Assured Arc Documentation**: Use when writing or maintaining README files, tutorials, architecture diagrams, agent documentation, skill documentation, or onboarding material. ([4R9UN/Assured-Arc](https://github.com/4R9UN/Assured-Arc/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/assured-arc-documentation.agent.md))
- **Assured Arc Orchestrator**: Global native harness coordinator. Builds an acceptance contract, selects relevant skills, composes disposable workers, enforces one writer, and returns a verified result. ([4R9UN/Assured-Arc](https://github.com/4R9UN/Assured-Arc/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/assured-arc-orchestrator.agent.md))
- **Assured Arc QA**: Use when reproducing bugs, designing tests, validating installer lifecycles, checking regression risk, or verifying a completed implementation. ([4R9UN/Assured-Arc](https://github.com/4R9UN/Assured-Arc/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/assured-arc-qa.agent.md))
- **Assured Arc Research**: Use when evaluating public extensions, agent skills, MCP servers, VS Code capabilities, Obsidian integrations, or external documentation before adoption. ([4R9UN/Assured-Arc](https://github.com/4R9UN/Assured-Arc/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/assured-arc-research.agent.md))
- **Assured Arc Security Review**: Use when reviewing installation scripts, MCP configuration, permissions, secrets handling, rollback behavior, or public release readiness. ([4R9UN/Assured-Arc](https://github.com/4R9UN/Assured-Arc/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/assured-arc-security-review.agent.md))
- **assured-arc-critic**: You are a zero-context Goldfish critic checking for blocking gaps, spec-to-code drift, unexamined trade-offs, code bloat, duplicate logic, or dead files. ([4R9UN/Assured-Arc](https://github.com/4R9UN/Assured-Arc/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/assured-arc-critic.md))
- **assured-arc-implementer**: You are the only writer for the assigned files. ([4R9UN/Assured-Arc](https://github.com/4R9UN/Assured-Arc/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/assured-arc-implementer.md))
- **assured-arc-orchestrator**: You coordinate one bounded task from the shared contract. ([4R9UN/Assured-Arc](https://github.com/4R9UN/Assured-Arc/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/assured-arc-orchestrator.md))
- **assured-arc-researcher**: You gather external and live web evidence for one bounded question. ([4R9UN/Assured-Arc](https://github.com/4R9UN/Assured-Arc/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/assured-arc-researcher.md))
- **assured-arc-scout**: You map the files, reusable modules, and constraints for one bounded task. ([4R9UN/Assured-Arc](https://github.com/4R9UN/Assured-Arc/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/assured-arc-scout.md))
- **assured-arc-verifier**: You verify a completed write against literal acceptance criteria. ([4R9UN/Assured-Arc](https://github.com/4R9UN/Assured-Arc/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/assured-arc-verifier.md))
- **ast-analyzer**: Specialized agent for generating Abstract Syntax Trees (AST) from source code, providing detailed structural analysis including classes, methods, statements, and dependencies ([Chris-Capgemini/test-custom-agents-2](https://github.com/Chris-Capgemini/test-custom-agents-2/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/ast-analyzer.agent.md))
- **ast-cco**: CCO (MrBeast)｜需要内容策略与编辑简报、平台内容适配、选题与脚本结构设计、内容制作编排或来源与权利审查时调用。典型信号：内容、选题、脚本、短视频、公众号、小红书、抖音、B站。 不适用：渠道投放与获客测量、产品与定价决策、登录账号或实际发布上传操作，或尚无已验证选题就要出成稿。 ([aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team](https://github.com/aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team/blob/HEAD/plugins/agi-super-team-claudecode/.claude/agents/ast-cco.md))
- **ast-cdo**: CDO (Nate Silver)｜需要数据契约与口径定义、数据质量与血缘治理、指标体系或分析模型设计、数据安全架构评审时调用。典型信号：数据口径、指标、数据质量、血缘、数据治理、报表。 不适用：研究方法设计与一手证据核验、具体业务决策取舍、生产应用与前端编码实现，或数据授权与合规依据尚未确认。 ([aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team](https://github.com/aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team/blob/HEAD/plugins/agi-super-team-claudecode/.claude/agents/ast-cdo.md))
- **ast-ceo**: CEO (Elon Musk)｜跨职能、公司级或高不确定性任务的总协调者。仅当任务跨两个以上 C-suite 领域、需要在资源/预算/范围/时间之间取舍，或多个专业结论冲突需要综合时调用；用户明确要求 CEO 或公司级决策也算。典型信号：公司级、跨职能、资源取舍、路线图、最终决策。 不适用：只涉及单一专业领域的判断、已由对应 C-suite 认领的执行细节、关键输入缺失就要拍板，或需要独立证据复核而非综合决策。 ([aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team](https://github.com/aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team/blob/HEAD/plugins/agi-super-team-claudecode/.claude/agents/ast-ceo.md))
- **ast-cfo**: CFO (Warren Buffett)｜需要预算与滚动预测、单位经济与定价证据、资本配置取舍、财务控制或成本优化时调用。典型信号：预算、定价、单位经济、成本、现金流、融资。 不适用：合同条款与合规判断、销售与客户关系推进、产品范围取舍，或需要实际付款、过账与税务申报操作。 ([aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team](https://github.com/aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team/blob/HEAD/plugins/agi-super-team-claudecode/.claude/agents/ast-cfo.md))
- **ast-clo**: CLO (Alan Dershowitz)｜需要合同与协议风险评审、隐私与跨境合规检查、许可与知识产权问题识别、监管政策适用判断时调用。典型信号：合同、隐私、合规、许可、知识产权、监管。 不适用：需要特定司法辖区的正式法律意见或诉讼代理、商业与财务取舍、技术安全实现，或交易结构与事实尚未明确。 ([aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team](https://github.com/aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team/blob/HEAD/plugins/agi-super-team-claudecode/.claude/agents/ast-clo.md))
- **ast-cmo**: CMO (David Ogilvy)｜需要市场定位与品牌策略、渠道增长实验、付费投放或 SEO 方案、获客测量与归因设计时调用。典型信号：品牌、获客、投放、广告、SEO、定位、归因。 不适用：内容选题、制作与渠道适配执行、产品功能与承诺取舍、定价与预算决策，或缺少受众与转化证据的品牌空转。 ([aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team](https://github.com/aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team/blob/HEAD/plugins/agi-super-team-claudecode/.claude/agents/ast-cmo.md))
- **ast-coo**: COO (Andy Grove)｜需要运营计划与执行节奏、责任与依赖映射、跨部门流程与 SOP 设计、产能规划或异常恢复安排时调用。典型信号：运营、SOP、流程、排期、产能、跨部门。 不适用：产品范围取舍、技术架构选型、财务预算与定价决策，或需要独立证据复核与放行门判定。 ([aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team](https://github.com/aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team/blob/HEAD/plugins/agi-super-team-claudecode/.claude/agents/ast-coo.md))
- **ast-cpo**: CPO (Steve Jobs)｜需要产品方向取舍、用户问题定义与需求澄清、体验与可访问性评审、优先级排序或可验证需求拆解时调用。典型信号：产品、需求、用户、体验、优先级、PRD、功能范围。 不适用：技术可行性与架构选型、获客渠道与投放执行、跨部门交付排期，或用户证据已充分只需界面与信息架构设计。 ([aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team](https://github.com/aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team/blob/HEAD/plugins/agi-super-team-claudecode/.claude/agents/ast-cpo.md))
- **ast-cqo**: CQO (Jim Simons)｜需要量化假设设计、回测与复现、模型评估与独立复核、组合风险控制或实验预登记时调用。典型信号：量化、回测、策略、因子、A股、选股、组合风险。 不适用：需要资本配置与预算决策、实盘下单或交易执行、经营现金流与利润预测，或问题缺少可复现数据与量化口径。 ([aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team](https://github.com/aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team/blob/HEAD/plugins/agi-super-team-claudecode/.claude/agents/ast-cqo.md))
- **ast-cro**: CRO (Richard Feynman)｜需要研究问题设计、一手证据获取与核验、竞品或市场情报扫描、多源证据综合与不确定性说明时调用。典型信号：研究、证据、竞品、调研、核验、市场情报。 不适用：需要财务、法律、产品或营销的最终结论、纯执行性的内容与投放工作、需要登录账号或联系第三方，或问题已有明确实验可直接验证。 ([aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team](https://github.com/aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team/blob/HEAD/plugins/agi-super-team-claudecode/.claude/agents/ast-cro.md))
- **ast-cso**: CSO (Michael Dell)｜需要客户发现与资格审查、赢单策略、方案与投标叙事、销售管道分析或客户成功交接设计时调用。典型信号：销售、客户、商机、赢单、投标、管道。 不适用：合同条款与合规判断、定价与单位经济测算、大规模需求生成与品牌投放，或尚无合格商机就要承诺交付与收入。 ([aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team](https://github.com/aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team/blob/HEAD/plugins/agi-super-team-claudecode/.claude/agents/ast-cso.md))
- **ast-cto**: CTO (Jensen Huang)｜需要技术战略选择、架构边界划分、接口/API 设计评审、技术选型对比、平台成本评估、系统可靠性设计或故障复盘时调用。典型信号：架构、技术选型、API/接口、性能、可靠性、故障复盘、系统设计。 不适用：实现层面的编码与集成、纯产品取舍、财务与法务判断、数据平台治理；非技术问题不得调用 CTO，也不要把 CTO 当作兜底角色。 ([aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team](https://github.com/aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team/blob/HEAD/plugins/agi-super-team-claudecode/.claude/agents/ast-cto.md))
- **ast-detector-writer**: Use when drafting a new tree-sitter-based detect.ast rule for guideline-checker (Python/TS/JS/JSX/TSX), including its proving fixture. Examples: &lt;example&gt;Context: user wants a new AST detector for a naming convention. user: "add a detector that flags snake_case React component files" assistant: "I'll use the ast-detector-writer agent to draft the tree-sitter query and a fixture proving it fires on a violation and not on a compliant file."&lt;/example&gt; ([chrysa/guideline-checker](https://github.com/chrysa/guideline-checker/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ast-detector-writer.md))
- **ast-governor**: Governor｜重大结论与完成声明的独立证据复核者。需要独立复核重大结论、发布、安全、资金、法律或完成声明，并给出放行门决定或风险升级时调用。典型信号：复核、放行、审计、风险升级、完成声明。 不适用：需要设计与实现交付物、替领域负责人作专业判断、替 CEO 作资源取舍，或声明方无法提供可复核证据。 ([aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team](https://github.com/aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team/blob/HEAD/plugins/agi-super-team-claudecode/.claude/agents/ast-governor.md))
- **ast-grep**: Use ast-grep for AST-aware structural code search when `rg` is too broad or too fragile. ([Insik-Han/han-monorepo-template](https://github.com/Insik-Han/han-monorepo-template/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/ast-grep.md) · ★17 · 2 copies)
- **ast-grep**: Use this agent when users need to perform AST-aware code searches, write structural code patterns, create ast-grep rules, or perform code transformations using syntax tree matching. This agent helps write correct ast-grep patterns and YAML rules for finding code structures like "functions without error handling" or "React components using specific hooks." Examples: &lt;example&gt; Context: User wants to find all console.log statements. user: "Find all console.log calls in my codebase" assistant: "I'll use the ast-grep agent to create a pattern for finding console.log calls." &lt;commentary&gt; Simple pat… ([pleaseai/code-intelligence](https://github.com/pleaseai/code-intelligence/blob/HEAD/agents/ast-grep.md))
- **ast-grep-developer**: Use this agent when you need to perform syntax-aware code search, refactoring, or transformations across codebases using AST-based patterns rather than plain text search. Examples: &lt;example&gt;Context: User wants to find and replace all instances of a specific function call pattern across their Rust codebase. user: 'I need to replace all .unwrap() calls with .expect() in my Rust project' assistant: 'I'll use the ast-grep-developer agent to perform this syntax-aware transformation safely' &lt;commentary&gt;Since the user needs AST-based code transformation, use the ast-grep-developer agent to handle th… ([Unintendedsideeffects/Skills-And-Agents](https://github.com/Unintendedsideeffects/Skills-And-Agents/blob/HEAD/agents/ast-grep-developer.md))

The agent who would have caught the round-9 MultiEdit matcher gap structurally — by reading the grammar, not by running the corpus. - **ast-pe**: PE (Linus Torvalds)｜已批准的设计需要跨模块实现、集成、测试、代码评审、缺陷修复或可回退交付时调用。典型信号：实现、编码、集成、测试、重构、缺陷修复、交付。 不适用：设计尚未批准、需要产品或优先级取舍、架构边界存在争议，或只需窄领域专家给出专项建议而不承担跨模块实现。 ([aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team](https://github.com/aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team/blob/HEAD/plugins/agi-super-team-claudecode/.claude/agents/ast-pe.md))
- **astack-agent**: Delegate role profile for code-writing and helper tasks spawned via `invoke_subagent` or `/swarm` during an `/astack-mode` run. Use for delegates, never as the orchestrator of `/astack-mode` itself. Spawn a fresh delegate for each distinct task (or continue an existing one via `send_message` only in the cases `astack-mode` names). Reads `skills/astack-mode/SKILL.md` in full, including its inline Principles index, before any work. ([jmxt3/astack](https://github.com/jmxt3/astack/blob/HEAD/agents/astack-agent.md) · ★2)
- **astack-comment-judge**: Review scoped comments against the keep clauses. Delete comments that fail them and report application-code changes as MUST KILL targets. ([vahaah/astack](https://github.com/vahaah/astack/blob/HEAD/dist/cursor/astack/agents/astack-comment-judge.md))
- **astack-delegate**: Delegate subagent for code and helper tasks during a `/astack-mode` run. Use for delegates, not as the runner for `/astack-mode` itself. Spawn a fresh `astack-delegate` for each new task. Resume one only in the strict cases that the mode's Subagents rules name. Read the full mode skill and the principles index before any work. A `general-purpose` substitute skips that read and drifts. ([vahaah/astack](https://github.com/vahaah/astack/blob/HEAD/dist/cursor/astack/agents/astack-delegate.md))
- **astack-explorer**: Read repo facts, review one diff axis, or outline an interface without changes. Used by astack-grilling, astack-code-review, astack-improve-codebase-architecture, and design-it-twice. ([vahaah/astack](https://github.com/vahaah/astack/blob/HEAD/dist/cursor/astack/agents/astack-explorer.md))
- **astack-postgres-reviewer**: Use for a requested read-only PostgreSQL review of a named diff. Reports verified behavior defects and proof limits. ([vahaah/astack](https://github.com/vahaah/astack/blob/HEAD/dist/cursor/astack/agents/astack-postgres-reviewer.md))
- **astack-researcher**: Research one question for astack-research or wayfinder. Write one cited Markdown file at the path or branch in the brief. ([vahaah/astack](https://github.com/vahaah/astack/blob/HEAD/dist/cursor/astack/agents/astack-researcher.md))
- **astack-typescript-reviewer**: Use for a requested read-only TypeScript and TSX review of a named diff. Reports verified behavior defects and proof limits. ([vahaah/astack](https://github.com/vahaah/astack/blob/HEAD/dist/cursor/astack/agents/astack-typescript-reviewer.md))
- **ASTD Debug Verifier**: Use when: debugging ASTD bugs, VFX parity issues, preview/runtime mismatch, automation failures, simulation failures, or when a subAgent must verify code results. ([KasumiNova/AsteriaDirectorate](https://github.com/KasumiNova/AsteriaDirectorate/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/astd-debug-verifier.agent.md) · ★3)
- **astera-reviewer**: Astera-specific read-only code review for Phase C close. Verifies Konsist green, application APIs use Result&lt;T,E&gt;, no hardcoded versions, i18n parity, ADR conventions, and that the wave did not touch off-limits paths. Reports violations; does not fix. ([AstarWorks/Astera](https://github.com/AstarWorks/Astera/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/astera-reviewer.md))
- **astera-worker**: Phase B implementation worker for Astera waves / cleanup passes. Implements one scoped territory (files, tests, YAML) handed over by the Opus orchestrator. Does not wire platform/Astera*Plugin.kt, write ADRs, run gradle, or touch git — it reports wiring needs back instead. - **asteroid-belt**: &gt; "An asteroid is not about size. It's about velocity and density. - **athena-point**: Athena 評分與分流器的 subagent 殼。**只供 athena-flow 呼叫**——main agent 不應該繞過 flow 直接調用此 subagent。執行時讀取 athena-point skill 並對 傳入的需求打分、寫 points/&lt;slug&gt;.md，然後回傳 verdict。 工具範圍刻意縮到 Read / Grep / Glob / Write（只能寫 points/）以及 受限的 Bash（**只允許**唯讀的 graphify query/path/explain 子指令， 用於在 codemap 可用時蒐集 codebase 結構線索）。 ([Athena-Git-Group/athena-plugin-dev](https://github.com/Athena-Git-Group/athena-plugin-dev/blob/HEAD/agents/athena-point.md))
- **athena-pre-reviewer**: House pre-submit reviewer for Athena working diffs. Use before opening any PR (pr-merge-loop step 2/3) — a correctness pass, attack-the-claims, a mutation check on every test-pinning claim, and, for risk-class fixes (resource bound, cap/limit, security guard, concurrency, escaping), the premise check. Reviews the diff, not the PR. ([GoodOlClint/Athena](https://github.com/GoodOlClint/Athena/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/athena-pre-reviewer.md))
- **athena-query-agent**: **Role:** Data Analyst & Lakehouse Operator ([Themis128/cloudless.gr](https://github.com/Themis128/cloudless.gr/blob/HEAD/.kiro/agents/athena-query-agent.md) · ★1)

([EdgarSocrates98/spark-forge-aws](https://github.com/EdgarSocrates98/spark-forge-aws/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/athena-query-optimizer.md) · 5 copies)
- **athena-specialist**: AWS Athena specialist for the stupid-db project. Deep expertise in querying historical data from S3 via Athena, SQL optimization for Athena, partition pruning, cost control, and integrating Athena query results with local materialization. Use for Athena queries, historical data analysis, or AWS integration work. - **athena-stage-build**: Build 階段的 subagent 殼（Minimal / Lightweight / Full phase）。**只供 athena-flow 呼叫**。載入 `.athena/skills/` 的團隊 build skill 實作，工具最廣 （Edit / Write / Bash 全開）但仍禁止 push 與危險 git 操作（沿用 deny list）。 Worktree 平行模式：開工先過 pre-flight 三項健檢（不符即停、回 `PRE-FLIGHT MISMATCH`、不寫 mini-handoff）；收尾 commit 到 worktree 分支並回報 `Worktree Branch:`。 ([Athena-Git-Group/athena-plugin-dev](https://github.com/Athena-Git-Group/athena-plugin-dev/blob/HEAD/agents/athena-stage-build.md))
- **athena-stage-plan**: Plan 階段的 subagent 殼。**只供 athena-flow 呼叫**。載入團隊在 `.athena/skills/` 下提供的 plan skill，將 spec 拆解為 phase cards 與 Dependency Graph。工具範圍：Read / Grep / Glob / Write（只能寫 plans/、handoffs/）+ 唯讀 Bash。不允許改動 src/。 ([Athena-Git-Group/athena-plugin-dev](https://github.com/Athena-Git-Group/athena-plugin-dev/blob/HEAD/agents/athena-stage-plan.md))
- **athena-stage-review**: Review 階段的 subagent 殼。**只供 athena-flow 呼叫**。載入團隊在 `.athena/skills/` 下提供的 review skill 做 code review。工具範圍： Read / Grep / Glob / Bash（靜態分析）+ Write（只能寫 handoffs/）。 **不允許 Edit**——review 是「指出問題」，不是「修問題」。 ([Athena-Git-Group/athena-plugin-dev](https://github.com/Athena-Git-Group/athena-plugin-dev/blob/HEAD/agents/athena-stage-review.md))
- **athena-stage-ship**: Ship 階段的 subagent 殼。**只供 athena-flow 呼叫**。執行 push 與 merge——這是流水線中唯一允許 push 的階段。工具範圍：Read / Bash (git push / git merge / gh cli) / Write（只能寫 handoffs/）。 **不允許 Edit**——ship 階段不該再改程式碼，那是 verify-fix 的工作。 ([Athena-Git-Group/athena-plugin-dev](https://github.com/Athena-Git-Group/athena-plugin-dev/blob/HEAD/agents/athena-stage-ship.md))
- **athena-stage-spec**: Spec 階段的 subagent 殼。**只供 athena-flow 呼叫**——main agent 不應該 繞過 flow 直接調用此 subagent。執行時載入 **flow 指定的** spec skill—— 來源二選一：團隊上繳在 `.athena/skills/` 下的，或 plugin 預設的 （`athena-spec-default`）——依其指示產出規格 artifact 與 handoffs/&lt;slug&gt;-spec.md。 工具範圍：Read / Grep / Glob / Write（只能寫 specs/、handoffs/）+ 少量 Bash（僅供查 git / 跑文件工具）。不允許改動 src/ 或執行任意 shell。 ([Athena-Git-Group/athena-plugin-dev](https://github.com/Athena-Git-Group/athena-plugin-dev/blob/HEAD/agents/athena-stage-spec.md))
- **athena-stage-verify**: Verify 階段的 subagent 殼。**只供 athena-flow 呼叫**。載入團隊在 `.athena/skills/` 下提供的 verify skill，驗證 build 產出。工具範圍： Read / Grep / Glob / Bash（跑測試與靜態檢查）+ Write（只能寫 handoffs/）。 **不允許 Edit / MultiEdit**——verify 看到問題應該回報，由 build agent 做 targeted re-build，不該自己悄悄改程式碼。 ([Athena-Git-Group/athena-plugin-dev](https://github.com/Athena-Git-Group/athena-plugin-dev/blob/HEAD/agents/athena-stage-verify.md))
